Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the condensed_matter framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 58. The resulting operator achieves bound O(867/n^5).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the condensed_matter decomposition. By minimality, the element a^58 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 867-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
